\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Relation-generic advance left-walk loop scaffold.  The
  chain over \isa{R{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}} is built natively by
  \isa{relpow{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}invariant{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}chain} from the per-step leaf contract
  supplied as the \isa{leaf} hypothesis; the union-relation
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}advance{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}walk{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}loop} and the sub-relation
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}advance{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}walk{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}loop{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}in{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}sub} are both thin instantiations,
  discharging \isa{leaf} by \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}advance{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}walk{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step} and
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}advance{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}walk{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}in{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}sub} respectively.  No
  \isa{stays} side-condition arises: the chain is native to
  \isa{R{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}} rather than lifted from the union relation, so the
  leaf-parametric scaffold applies
  where a union-to-sub chain lift would not.
  Throughout, \isa{b} abbreviates \isa{block{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}width\ {\isasymGamma}} (the per-symbol cell width).%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The advance left-walk loop, proper tape.  From an
  \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimAdvance} stage at bit-counter \isa{{\isadigit{0}}} with the head
  at \isa{start}, \isa{n} \isa{M{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}}-steps walk the head
  \isa{n} cells \isa{L} (counter \isa{{\isadigit{0}}\ {\isasymrightarrow}\ n}), the tape
  and all other heads unchanged.  Each \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}advance{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}walk{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}'s
  \isa{{\isasymnoteq}\ LE{\isadigit{4}}} guard reads the current cell
  \isa{start\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ j}, supplied \isa{{\isasymnoteq}\ LE{\isadigit{4}}} by \isa{notLE};
  the per-step displacement bound \isa{Suc\ j\ {\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ D} follows from
  \isa{n\ {\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ D} by \isa{linarith}.  Unlike the write loops this loop
  does not mutate the tape (advance only moves heads), so the invariant
  keeps \isa{mt{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape\ c\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ mt{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape\ c{\isadigit{0}}} constant.  Chain length
  \isa{n}.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
\isakeywordONE{lemma}\isamarkupfalse%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Relation-generic unified single-tape advance scaffold
  (non-last tape).  Both helper references of the dispatch ---
  the walk loop and the boundary step --- are abstracted as the
  \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}loop} and \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}boundary} hypotheses, so the
  union-relation \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}advance{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step} and the sub-relation
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}advance{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}in{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}sub} are both thin instantiations
  (discharging the two leaves by the union- resp. sub-relation
  helper lemmas).  Multi-leaf instance of the leaf-parametric
  scaffold; no \isa{stays}
  side-condition since both leaves' chains are native to \isa{R{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}}.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The unified single-tape advance, non-last tape: the
  \isa{R}/non-\isa{R} dispatch, the advance analogue of
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}.  From an \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimAdvance} stage at
  bit-counter \isa{{\isadigit{0}}}, tape \isa{tk}'s head is repositioned to
  \isa{M}'s new cell and the hand-off goes to the next tape
  \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}succ\ tk} (counter \isa{{\isadigit{0}}}, \isa{posk\ tk} updated by
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}newpos}).  An \isa{R}-move needs no head motion (the
  head already sits at \isa{sim{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}pos\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}p\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}}): a single boundary
  step, cost \isa{{\isadigit{1}}}.  A non-\isa{R}-move walks the head
  \isa{D\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}disp} cells \isa{L}: the \isa{D\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}}-step
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}advance{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}walk{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}loop} followed by the final boundary
  \isa{L}-move, cost \isa{D}, landing the head at
  \isa{start\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ D}.  The \isa{dge{\isadigit{1}}} hypothesis rules out the
  forbidden \isa{L}-from-\isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}AtLE} (zero displacement) so that
  \isa{D\ {\isasymge}\ {\isadigit{1}}}; \isa{notLE} covers every cell the walk reads
  (\isa{start\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ m}, \isa{m\ {\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ D}).  The tape is unchanged; only
  the head on \isa{tk} moves.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Relation-generic unified single-tape advance scaffold
  (last tape).  As \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}advance{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}gen} but the boundary
  leaf transitions to \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimNext}; both helper references
  (walk loop, finish step) are the \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}loop} and
  \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}finish} hypotheses, so the union- and sub-relation
  versions are thin instantiations.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The unified single-tape advance, last tape: as
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}advance{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step} but \isa{tk} is the last tape, so the
  boundary step (\isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}advance{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}finish{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}) transitions to
  \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimNext} (current-tape field reset to \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unidx\ {\isadigit{0}}})
  rather than advancing to \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}succ\ tk}.  Same \isa{R}/non-\isa{R}
  dispatch, the same walk-then-boundary decomposition, and the same
  conditional head conclusion.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The advance prefix walk: a custom induction on the tape index
  \isa{j} (\isa{j\ {\isasymle}\ k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}}, so every tape it touches is
  non-last), iterating \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}advance{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step} from the boundary
  tape \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unidx\ {\isadigit{0}}}.  Like the read prefix, the tape is constant and the carried state is
  a \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}idx\ k\ {\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ j} split: tapes already advanced
  (\isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}idx\ k\ {\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ j}) hold their \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}newpos}-updated
  position-kind and their repositioned head, the rest hold boundary
  values.  The per-tape \isa{notLE}/\isa{dge{\isadigit{1}}} entry facts hold of
  the current tape \isa{{\isacharquery}{\kern0pt}tk} because it is not yet visited
  (\isa{{\isacharquery}{\kern0pt}pk\ j\ {\isacharquery}{\kern0pt}tk\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ posk{\isadigit{0}}\ {\isacharquery}{\kern0pt}tk}, \isa{mt{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}pos\ c\ {\isacharquery}{\kern0pt}tk\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ mt{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}pos\ c{\isadigit{0}}\ {\isacharquery}{\kern0pt}tk}).
  Aggregate cost \isa{{\isasymle}\ j\ {\isasymcdot}\ {\isadigit{2}}b} (each per-tape move is at most
  \isa{{\isadigit{2}}b} cells, \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}disp{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}le{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}{\isadigit{2}}k}).%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Relation-generic full advance phase scaffold.  The two
  helpers (prefix walk, last-tape finish) are the \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}prefix}
  and \isa{leaf{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}finish} hypotheses; the union- and sub-relation
  phases are thin instantiations.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

The full advance phase: the prefix walk over the first
  \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}} tapes followed by the unified last-tape advance
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}advance{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}finish{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step}, landing at \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimNext}
  (current-tape field \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unidx\ {\isadigit{0}}}) with every tape's head moved to
  \isa{M}'s new position (\isa{R}: unchanged; otherwise
  \isa{{\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}disp}) and every \isa{posk} updated by
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}newpos}.  The tape is unchanged throughout (advance only
  moves heads).  Aggregate cost \isa{{\isasymle}\ k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M\ {\isasymcdot}\ {\isadigit{2}}b}.  The
  final \isa{posk}/position vectors collapse from the
  \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}idx\ k\ {\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharquery}{\kern0pt}m{\isadigit{1}}} split because, on the last tape, every other
  tape has index \isa{{\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}} (\isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}idx} bijective into
  \isa{{\isacharbraceleft}{\kern0pt}{\isachardot}{\kern0pt}{\isachardot}{\kern0pt}{\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M{\isacharbraceright}{\kern0pt}}).%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
